// EEL division by zero returns zero; RNBO arithmetic does not.
function eelDivide(a,b) { return b == 0 ? 0 : a/b; }
function fn__sli(arg_id, arg_mi, arg_ma, arg_ze) {
let tmp5: Int = trunc(s_slimi+(arg_id));
ram[tmp5] = arg_mi;
let tmp6: Int = trunc(s_slima+(arg_id));
ram[tmp6] = arg_ma;
let tmp7: Int = trunc(s_slize+(arg_id));
ram[tmp7] = arg_ze;
return ram[tmp7];
}

function fn__upsli() {
let tmp68 = 1;
let tmp69 = (slider1 * .02);
let tmp70 = 0;
let tmp71 = ((slider1 * .02) - 1);
s_roomsize = pow(((pow(minimum(tmp68, tmp69),.5) * .5) + (pow(maximum(tmp70, tmp71),1.4) * .5)),.8);
let tmp72 = 1;
let tmp73 = (slider2 * .02);
let tmp74 = 0;
let tmp75 = ((slider2 * .02) - 1);
let tmp76 = .1;
let tmp77 = s_roomsize;
s_decay = pow(((pow(minimum(tmp72, tmp73),.7) * .5) + (pow(maximum(tmp74, tmp75),1.3) * .5)),(.3 + pow(maximum(tmp76, tmp77),.5)));
s_decays = ((.85 + (pow(s_roomsize,(3 - (pow(s_decay,.75) * 2.5))) * 22)) * (0.07 + pow(s_decay,2.8)));
s_dampen = pow((slider3 * .01),1);
s_predelms = slider4;
s_attack = (.5 + (pow((slider5 * .01),.7) * .5));
s_lpfk = slider6;
s_notch3db = slider7;
s_notch3f = slider8;
s_notch2db = slider9;
s_notch2f = slider10;
s_notch1db = slider11;
s_notch1f = slider12;
s_hpf = slider13;
s_width = (slider14 * .01);
s_mix = pow((slider15 * .01),1.2);
s_voldb = slider16;
let tmp78 = (s_mix - .5);
s_mixvol = eelDivide(1,(1 + ((abs(tmp78) + .5) * .15)));
let tmp79 = 1;
let tmp80 = (s_mix * 2);
s_mixwet = ((minimum(tmp79, tmp80) * s_mixvol) * .7);
let tmp81 = 1;

function fn_L_cycleEnd1() {
